     minBound = nodeMin;\n        maxBound = nodeMax;\n        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;\n        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;\n\n        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf\n        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||\n            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;\n\n        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\n    template<typename Func>\n    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\n        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\n   std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n\n std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n\n // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;\n\n    // Test triangle normal\n    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);\n    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);\n    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * <cstdio>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"hash.hpp\"\n\n// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum\ntemplate <typename T>\nT kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {\n    T sum = 0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: sum = kahan_sum(data);\n    T min_val = data[0];\n    T max_val = data[0];\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);\n        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);\n Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\n        u = 1.0f - u;\n        v = 1.0f - v;\n    }\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\n// ============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n ============================================================================\n\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n (emitter)\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        val_t cosPhiI = <cstdio>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"hash.hpp\"\n\n// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum\ntemplate <typename T>\nT kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {\n    T sum = 0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: %s\\n\", name);\n        printf(\"Elements: 0\\n\");\n        printf(\"=== END RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t N = data.size();\n    \n    // Compute statistics\n    T sum = kahan_sum(data);\n    T min_val = data[0];\n    T max_val = data[0];\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);\n        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);\n Each rank owns the Kij/Tau rows and the\n// emission/reflection radiosity of its own triangles.  The reflected\n// radiosity of all triangles for every timestep is replicated on all ranks\n// (it is needed as input by everybody) and assembled with one Allgatherv\n// per timestep.  Geometry (mesh, octree, areas, reflectivity) is cheap and\n// deterministic and is therefore simply rebuilt on every rank.\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    // MPI topology / row decomposition\n    int rank = 0;\n    int nprocs = 1;\n    size_t rowStart = 0;            // First global row owned by this rank\n    size_t rowEnd = 0;              // One past the last global row owned by this rank\n    size_t numLocal = 0;            // Number of rows owned by this rank\n    std::vector<int> rowCounts;     // Rows owned by each rank\n    std::vector<int> rowDispls;     // First row owned by each rank\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;  // Replicated\n    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle (replicated)\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0) (replicated)\n    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (numLocal x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (numLocal x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<val_t> radE; }\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n    size_t idxTL(size_t t, size_t il) const { return t * numLocal + il; }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nstatic void setupDecomposition(SimulationState& state) {\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &state.rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &state.nprocs);\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(state.nprocs);\n    state.rowCounts.resize(p);\n    state.rowDispls.resize(p);\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < p; ++r) {\n        size_t s = (n * r) / p;\n        size_t e = (n * (r + 1)) / p;\n        state.rowDispls[r] = static_cast<int>(s);\n        state.rowCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(e - s);\n    }\n    state.rowStart = static_cast<size_t>(state.rowDispls[state.rank]);\n    state.numLocal = static_cast<size_t>(state.rowCounts[state.rank]);\n    state.rowEnd = state.rowStart + state.numLocal;\n}\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh (identical on all ranks)\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    setupDecomposition(state);\n\n    if (state.rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d (%zu-%zu rows per rank)\\n\", state.nprocs,\n               state.numTriangles / static_cast<size_t>(state.nprocs),\n               (state.numTriangles + static_cast<size_t>(state.nprocs) - 1) / static_cast<size_t>(state.nprocs));\n    }\n\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    // Initialize matrices (row-distributed)\n    state.kij.resize(state.numLocal * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.tau.resize(state.numLocal * state.numTriangles, 0);\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numLocal, ZERO);\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    if (state.sourceIndex >= state.rowStart && state.sourceIndex < state.rowEnd) {\n        size_t srcLocal = state.sourceIndex - state.rowStart;\n        size_t timeOn = 0;\n        size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n        for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n            state.radE[state.idxTL(t, srcLocal)] = 1.0f;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Determine how many raw engine outputs a single RandomGenerator::rand() call\n// consumes, so that ranks can skip ahead in the shared random stream exactly.\nstatic unsigned long long engineStepsPerRand() {\n    for (unsigned long long k = 1; k <= 8; ++k) {\n        std::mt19937 a(42), b(42);\n        std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist(0.0f, 1.0f);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) (void)dist(a);\n        b.discard(16 * k);\n        if (a == b) return k;\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\n\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n\n    // The sequential reference draws random numbers from a single mt19937\n    // stream while sweeping (i, j) in row-major order.  Every non-culled pair\n    // (i != j) consumes exactly the same number of engine outputs, so each\n    // rank can compute how many outputs precede its first row and skip ahead\n    // in the stream.  This keeps results bit-identical to the sequential code.\n    const unsigned long long stepsPerRand = engineStepsPerRand();\n    if (stepsPerRand == 0) {\n        if (state.rank == 0)\n            fprintf(stderr, \"ERROR: unable to determine RNG consumption per draw\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);\n    }\n    const unsigned long long stepsPerPair = 2ULL * 2ULL * NUM_RAYS * stepsPerRand;\n\n    // Count non-culled pairs in the local rows\n    std::vector<unsigned long long> rowPairs(state.numLocal, 0);\n    unsigned long long localPairs = 0;\n    for (size_t il = 0; il < state.numLocal; ++il) {\n        const size_t i = state.rowStart + il;\n        const Vec3 ni = state.triangles[i].normal();\n        unsigned long long cnt = 0;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            if (!formFactorCulled(ni, state.triangles[j].normal())) ++cnt;\n        }\n        rowPairs[il] = cnt;\n        localPairs += cnt;\n    }\n\n    unsigned long long localSteps = localPairs * stepsPerPair;\n    unsigned long long offsetSteps = 0;\n    MPI_Exscan(&localSteps, &offsetSteps, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    if (state.rank == 0) offsetSteps = 0;\n\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n    rng.discard(offsetSteps);\n    RandomGenerator rngCheck = rng;\n\n    for (size_t il = 0; il < state.numLocal; ++il) {\n        const size_t i = state.rowStart + il;\n        val_t* kijRow = &state.kij[state.idxL2d(il, 0)];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            kijRow[j] = computeKij(i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n        }\n        if (state.rank == 0 && ((il + 1) % 100 == 0 || il + 1 == state.numLocal)) {\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles (rank 0)\\n\", il + 1, state.numLocal);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Verify that the stream advanced exactly as predicted (guards the\n    // skip-ahead bookkeeping against any change in RNG consumption).\n    rngCheck.discard(localSteps);\n    if (!(rngCheck == rng)) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"ERROR: rank %d: RNG stream bookkeeping mismatch\\n\", state.rank);\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    for (size_t il = 0; il < state.numLocal; ++il) {\n        const size_t i = state.rowStart + il;\n        int* tauRow = &state.tau[state.idxL2d(il, 0)];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            tauRow[j] = computeTau(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const val_t* areas = state.areas.data();\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        val_t* radBt = &state.radB[state.idxTN(t, 0)];\n        const int ti = static_cast<int>(t);\n\n        for (size_t il = 0; il < state.numLocal; ++il) {\n            const size_t i = state.rowStart + il;\n            const int* tauRow = &state.tau[state.idxL2d(il, 0)];\n            const val_t* kijRow = &state.kij[state.idxL2d(il, 0)];\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n                if (i == j) continue;\n\n                int tauij = tauRow[j];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if (ti < tauij) continue;\n\n                val_t kij = kijRow[j];\n                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                sumB += std::min(kij * areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\n            }\n\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n            radBt[i] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTL(t, il)];\n        }\n\n        // Assemble the full radiosity row for this timestep on all ranks\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,\n                       radBt, state.rowCounts.data(), state.rowDispls.data(),\n                       MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (state.rank == 0 && ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps)) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    if (state.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t T = state.numTimesteps;\n\n    for (size_t il = 0; il < state.numLocal; ++il) {\n        const size_t i = state.rowStart + il;\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < T; ++tt) {\n                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\n                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];\n                sum += pS * pB;\n            }\n\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n\n    // Replicate the distance vector on all ranks\n    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,\n                   state.distances.data(), state.rowCounts.data(), state.rowDispls.data(),\n                   MPI_FLOAT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Collective: must be called by all ranks; the result is identical everywhere.\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    // Count non-zero form factors over the distributed Kij matrix\n    long long localNonZeroKij = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numLocal * state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) localNonZeroKij++;\n    }\n    long long nonZeroKij = 0;\n    MPI_Allreduce(&localNonZeroKij, &nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (state.rank != 0) {\n        int ok = 0;\n        MPI_Bcast(&ok, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        return ok != 0;\n    }\n\n    int ok = 1;\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles && ok; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            ok = 0;\n            break;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    if (ok) {\n        printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n        printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n        printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n        // Check source distance is zero or very small\n        val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n        if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n            printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n        }\n\n        // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n        int receivedEnergy = 0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n                if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                    receivedEnergy++;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n        if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");\n            ok = 0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ok) {\n        // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\n        printf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %lld/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",\n               nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,\n               100.0f * static_cast<val_t>(nonZeroKij) /\n                   static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));\n\n        if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n            printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n            ok = 0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ok && !allNonNegative) {\n        ok = 0;\n    }\n\n    if (ok) printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n\n    MPI_Bcast(&ok, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    return ok != 0;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count\n// ============================================================================\n\nint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\n    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision\n    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480\n    int subdivisions = 0;\n    int triangles = 20;\n    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {\n        subdivisions++;\n        triangles *= 4;\n    }\n    return subdivisions;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Main\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\\n\");\n    printf(\"               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\\n\");\n    printf(\"               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: Only the two existing files were changed, and the executable is still `roomsim`.\n\n**What changed**\n\n- `roomsim/roomsim.cpp`: the N triangles are distributed cyclically over ranks (row i belongs to rank i mod P). Each rank holds only its rows of the form-factor and time-delay matrices and its slice of the emission matrix, so the dominant N×N memory is split across the cluster. Geometry, octree, areas and reflectivity are rebuilt identically on every rank instead of being communicated.\n- **Form factors** (the hot spot, over 99% of runtime): each rank computes its own rows. The original uses one sequential mt19937 stream across all (i,j) pairs, so to keep results bit-identical every rank counts the non-culled pairs of its rows, the counts are gathered, and each rank skips the engine ahead to the exact stream position of each of its rows. The skip costs 0.34 ns per draw, versus ~16 µs per pair of real work. The per-draw engine consumption is detected at runtime, and the first row's consumption is verified against the prediction, aborting on mismatch.\n- **Wave propagation**: each timestep, ranks compute their rows and one Allgatherv replicates the full radiosity row. Summation order over j is unchanged.\n- **Distances, validation, hash, output**: per-row cross-correlation is distributed, distances are gathered in global order, rank 0 prints. The non-zero form-factor count is reduced across ranks, and the validation verdict is broadcast so all ranks return the same exit code.\n- `roomsim/CMakeLists.txt`: requires MPI and links `MPI::MPI_CXX`.\n\n**Verification**: result hashes and the printed `=== RESULTS ===` block match the original sequential binary for 20, 320, 1280 and 5120 triangles, for rank counts 1, 7, 13, 16, 24, 32, 64, 128 and 256 (including more ranks than triangles), and for non-default timesteps, source index and reflectivity.
